## 0. THE OPERATING RULES OF THIS DELTA AUDIT

**[P]** Thirty-one rules are stated here; a thirty-second, **DA-32**, is minted at §XIV.1 where the need for it first arises — **32 in total**. Each is either carried forward verbatim from the corpus and its instruments, or is required by the mandate for this audit. Every Part states which rules bind it.

### 0.2 Rules new to this audit

| ID | **Rule** | **Why it is required** |
|---|---|---|
| **DA-25** | **An assertion of supply is not a supply.** Where a document is declared to have been supplied and the intake does not contain it, the record states both facts — the declaration and the absence — and populates no field from either | **Track 2. Without this rule the audit would either fabricate a receipt or accuse the founder of a false statement. It does neither** |
| **DA-26** | **A negative declaration is evidence about the record, and is recorded at the level of the declarant.** *"No legal/constitutive document exists"* from the only candidate holder is `CONFIRMED ABSENT (E1)`. It is not `CONFIRMED ABSENT (E4)`, and it is not a silence | **KDP-03. The mandate requires that a nonexistent document stop being requested as though it were merely missing** |
| **DA-27** | **A founder declaration establishes what the founder declares, and nothing adjacent to it.** *"Owns and controls Krytos"* does not establish ownership of Sewak Sathi, of the brand, of the IP, of the data, of an account, or of anything Krytos is said to own | **§IV.4, §VII. Adjacency is the dominant failure mode of disclosure processing** |
| **DA-28** | **A new term is recorded as a new term.** Where the founder introduces vocabulary that occurs zero times in the corpus, the term is recorded verbatim, its corpus frequency is recorded as zero, and it is not mapped onto an existing term, a legal category or a relationship model | ***"Virtual Brain (VB)"* and *"ecosystem head"* — 0 occurrences each. §II.3, §IV.1, Part XXI U-PD-3** |
| **DA-29** | **A gate moves only on its own PASS condition.** No gate is recorded as moved because an input to it improved, because a blocker was explained, or because a professional could now be instructed | **Part XVII. 0 of 10 P0 sub-gates move. The audit states why each fails in its own words** |
| **DA-30** | **No authority by disclosure.** A person who supplies facts does not thereby acquire authority to decide what the facts mean. **AS-1, AS-2 and AS-3 remain separate fields** | **§III.3. The founder has answered AS-1; AS-2 and AS-3 are not answered by the same act** |
| **DA-31** | **This audit produces no successor instrument.** It records what the next human action is and stops. If that action is professional work, it says so; if it is a founder decision, it says so; if it is another upload, it says so | **§XXVI. [S-K: Rule K-69] and Part XXVI §XXVI.4 of [S-K] are honoured: the loop does not restart because evidence arrived** |

# PART II — INTAKE VERIFICATION: TWO TRACKS, TWO STATES

## II.0 Method

**[P]** Bound by **DA-1, DA-4, DA-5, DA-9, DA-25, DA-26, DA-28**.

**[P]** The mandate declares two new inputs. **They are verified separately, by inspection, and they are not in the same state.** The intake pattern **IN-U-1 … IN-U-10** is run in full because five consecutive generations produced an empty intake and the sixth must not be assumed either way.

| Step | **Inspection performed** | **Why** |
|---|---|---|
| **IN-U-1** | Enumerate every file in the repository root | The archive and the five instruments are the pre-existing corpus |
| **IN-U-2** | `sha256sum` the archive and compare against the recorded value | Detect any substitution of the corpus |
| **IN-U-3** | `unzip -l` the archive; count entries | Detect any addition to the corpus |
| **IN-U-4** | Enumerate the declared attachment path `/home/user/uploads/` | The mandate's own declared location for the new file |
| **IN-U-5** | Filesystem-wide search for the declared filename, for `*.pdf`, and for `*blueprint*` (case-insensitive) | A file delivered to any other location must still be found |
| **IN-U-6** | Filesystem-wide search for any file modified since the previous generation, excluding `/proc`, `/sys`, `/usr`, `/snap`, `/var/log`, `/var/lib` and `.git` | Detect any arrival under a different name |
| **IN-U-7** | Enumerate mount points and `/tmp`, `/var/tmp`, `/tmp/arena-workspace` | Detect a delivered-but-unmounted volume |
| **IN-U-8** | Re-extract the archive to `/tmp/corpus` and confirm line counts | The corpus is inspected directly, never from memory |
| **IN-U-9** | Search the corpus for the founder's three new terms | Establish whether the declaration uses corpus vocabulary |
| **IN-U-10** | Search the corpus for the founder's surname | Establish whether the person is newly identified or already identified |

|---|---|

## II.2 IN-U-4 … IN-U-7: the declared attachment

**[P]** The mandate names one new source document: **`Zero_Capital_Local_Venture_Ecosystem_Blueprint.pdf`**, and states that it has been supplied as an actual file. **The intake was inspected for it six ways. It is not there.**

| # | **Inspection** | **Command class** | **Result** |
|---|---|---|---|
| **1** | The declared attachment directory | Enumerate `/home/user/uploads/` | **`No such file or directory`. The directory does not exist** |
| **2** | The declared filename, anywhere | Filesystem-wide search for `*Zero_Capital*`, excluding `/proc` | **0 matches** |
| **3** | Any PDF, anywhere | Filesystem-wide search for `*.pdf`, excluding `/proc`, `/sys`, `/usr`, `/snap` | **0 matches outside the archive** — the corpus's own `uploads/…v1_2.pdf` exists only *inside* the zip, and is extracted to `/tmp/corpus/uploads/` |
| **4** | Any file whose name contains "blueprint" | Filesystem-wide case-insensitive search | **1 match — the filename of [S-J] itself**, `…Economic_Blueprint_Recovery_Register.md`. **No source document** |
| **5** | Any file modified since the previous generation | Filesystem-wide, excluding system and log paths | **6 matches — the archive and the five delivered instruments. No arrival** |
| **6** | Any delivered-but-unmounted volume | Enumerate mounts; enumerate `/tmp`, `/var/tmp`, `/tmp/arena-workspace` | **Root filesystem only (`/dev/vda on /`). `/tmp/arena-workspace` is empty. No additional mount** |

> ### **TRACK 2 RESULT: `FILE NOT PRESENT IN INTAKE`**
>
> **`Zero_Capital_Local_Venture_Ecosystem_Blueprint.pdf` was declared supplied and did not arrive. Six inspections, six negative results.**
>
> **This audit records the declaration and records the absence, and populates no field from either (DA-25). It does not characterise the declaration as false: a transfer may have failed, a path may have been wrong, an upload may not have completed, or the file may have been intended and not attached. Which of these occurred is `NOT ESTABLISHED` and is not inferred.**

**[A] Finding U-PD-1 (new).** **The corpus has now recorded the same document's absence in three different states across six generations, and the three states are not equivalent.**

| Generation | **The state of the blueprint** | **What the record could say** |
|---|---|---|
| **v1.3-G … v1.3-I** | **`SOURCE-REFERENCED-NOT-SUPPLIED`** | A title is quoted at **[appendix.txt:682–683]**; no holder is named; nothing is known about the file |
| **v1.3-J … v1.3-K** | **`SOURCE-REFERENCED-NOT-SUPPLIED`, with `U17-R2 NOT MET` — *"no holder is named anywhere"*** | The absence is now located: not only is the file missing, **nobody is identified who has it** |
| **This audit** | **`SOURCE-ASSERTED-SUPPLIED — NOT PRESENT IN INTAKE`, with `U17-R2 MET AT E1`** | **A holder is identified and asserts that the file was supplied. The absence has moved from *"nobody has it"* to *"the holder says he sent it and it did not arrive"*. That is a transfer failure, not a knowledge failure — and it is a materially different next action** |

**Consequence, stated exactly: the blueprint requirement is no longer an acquisition problem. It is a delivery problem.** Part XI §XI.5 and Part XXVI.

> **This is the mandate's own instruction, executed: *"Do not continue asking for a nonexistent document as though it is merely missing."* From this audit forward, `REQ-A-v1.0` items **A-03.2** and **A-03.3** are closed by an express negative and must not be re-issued. **A-03.1** survives in one narrowed form only: the four referenced **architecture** documents **KP-40, KP-41, KP-43, KP-44** are *not* legal/constitutive documents, and FA-4 does not address them. §IV.3.*

## II.5 A new response state — `RS-9`

**[P]** **[S-K: §VII.1]** defines eight response states. **None describes Track 2.** `RS-1 DOCUMENT SUPPLIED` requires a document. `RS-6 REFERENCE ONLY` covers *"a document is named, described or cited; the document does not arrive"* — which is close, but understates the case: **here the respondent does not merely name the document, he states that he supplied it.** `RS-7 UNREADABLE / INCOMPLETE` requires material to have arrived. A ninth state is therefore minted, with its own level and its own FSM behaviour, and with no implied blame.

| ID | **State** | **Definition** | **What it establishes** | **Level** | **FSM behaviour** | **Instances today** |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **RS-9** *(new)* | **`ASSERTED SUPPLIED — NOT PRESENT IN INTAKE`** | A respondent states that a document has been supplied; inspection of the intake does not find it | **Two facts, both recorded: that the respondent asserts delivery, and that the intake does not contain the item.** Nothing about the document's content, existence, authenticity or authority | **E1** as to the assertion of delivery; **E0** as to content | **FSM-04 → FSM-05 → stops.** The item is classified and does not advance a field **(DA-25, K-5)**. The correct next action is **re-transfer and re-inspection**, not re-request | **1 — `EV-B-01`** |

**[P] `RS-9` is distinguished from `RS-6` and from `RS-8` on the record:**

| | **RS-6 `REFERENCE ONLY`** | **RS-8 `NO RESPONSE`** | **RS-9 `ASSERTED SUPPLIED — NOT PRESENT`** |
|---|---|---|---|
| **What the respondent said** | Named or cited a document | Nothing | **"I supplied it"** |
| **What arrived** | Nothing | Nothing | Nothing |
| **What is known about the holder** | May be unknown | Unknown | **Identified, and asserts possession** |
| **Correct next action** | Request the document | Re-issue the request | **Verify and re-attempt the transfer** |
| **Corpus instances before this audit** | **5 — KP-40, KP-41, KP-42's referenced v1.0 establishment, KP-43, KP-44's file** *(K-PD-8)* | **31 of 31 SA rows · 36 of 36 sub-items** | **0** |
| **Instances now** | **5 — unchanged. FA-7 does not supply KP-40 … KP-44** | **10 of 36 sub-items remain unanswered; 21 are touched** | **1** |

## II.6 A state-machine anomaly, recorded rather than smoothed

**[P]** **[S-K: §XXIV.2]** defines an eleven-state machine whose only entry to **FSM-04 `RESPONSE RECEIVED`** is **FSM-03 `REQUEST ISSUED`**, and whose Rule K-67 forbids shortcuts. **That is not what happened.**

| State | **Position before this audit** | **Position now** | **Note** |
|---|---|---|---|
| **FSM-01 `NOT REQUESTED`** | **31 of 31 SA rows** | **10 of 31 SA rows remain here; 21 are touched by an unsolicited disclosure** | **SA-30, SA-31 and the eight rows the seven assertions do not reach are unchanged** |
| **FSM-02 `RECIPIENT IDENTIFIED`** | **Not reached — `NO RECIPIENT IDENTIFIED`; STOP-09** | **`REACHED — BY DISCLOSURE, NOT BY SELECTION`.** REC-01 is populated at §II.7 | **The machine's entry event was *"a person with standing is identified and recorded in REC-01"* — satisfied** |
| **FSM-03 `REQUEST ISSUED`** | **Not reached — `REQ-A-v1.0` and `REQ-B-v1.0` both `COMPOSED — NOT ISSUED`** | **`BYPASSED — NO REQUEST WAS ISSUED`.** **AUD-03 remains `NOT ISSUED`** | **Rule K-68 stands: composition did not advance the state, and neither did bypass** |
| **FSM-04 `RESPONSE RECEIVED`** | **Not reached — 39 of 39 rows at `RS-8`** | **`REACHED — UNSOLICITED`.** Seven assertions received, of one response state `RS-2`, two `RS-3`, one `RS-9` | **The machine has no transition into FSM-04 that does not pass FSM-03. **U-PD-6** records the anomaly instead of rewriting the machine** |
| **FSM-05 `DOCUMENT / ASSERTION CLASSIFIED`** | **Not reached** | **`REACHED` for all seven assertions and for `EV-B-01`.** Classification is Part III | **`RS-9` stops here, as `RS-6` does** |
| **FSM-06 `PROVENANCE CHECK`** | **Not reached** | **`REACHED` — P1 for the assertions, P0 for the declared file. AU-01 … AU-10 applied to no document, because no document exists** | **No item advances past P1** |
| **FSM-07 `AUTHORITY CHECK`** | **Not reached** | **`REACHED` — AS-1, AS-2, AS-3 separated at §III.3. A-05 classified `ASSERTED`** | **Standing is asserted, not established** |
| **FSM-08 `CONFLICT CHECK`** | **Not reached** | **`REACHED` — two new conflicts opened, **CF-21** and **CF-22**; twenty pre-existing conflicts re-tested at Part VII. **Neither new conflict is resolved inside this audit** *(K-10, [S-K: §X.2])* | **The machine holds here for the conflicted items** |
| **FSM-09 `VALIDATION QUEUE`** | **Not reached — 0 of 52 commissionable** | **`REACHED FOR THE FIRST TIME` — Rule K-58's four conditions are tested at Part XIX. **19 of 52 become `FORMULABLE — E1`; 0 are commissioned** | **Condition ④ passes for the first time in six generations** |
| **FSM-10 `DECISION QUEUE`** | **Not reached — `DQ-01` unused** | **`NOT REACHED`.** No validation has been obtained; **DL-3, DL-4, DL-5 still have no decider** | **`DQ-01` remains unused** |
| **FSM-11 `RETURN TO ARCHITECTURE`** | **Not reached — 0 returns** | **`NOT REACHED` as a decision return.** Per-field evidence recording is performed, which Rule K-54 permits and which is not an architectural decision | **0 decisions recorded** |

**[A] Finding U-PD-6 (new).** **The machine was entered from outside.** **FSM-04 was reached without FSM-03, because the disclosure was unsolicited: nobody issued `REQ-A-v1.0`, and the founder spoke anyway.** This is not a defect in the machine and not a defect in the disclosure. It is a fact about how the evidence arrived, and it has three consequences that are recorded rather than smoothed:

1. **`AUD-03` cannot record an issuance.** The audit trail's issuance class remains empty; a new class is required for unsolicited disclosure and is added at §XX.3 as **`AUD-11 UNSOLICITED DISCLOSURE RECEIVED`**.
2. **The neutrality discipline of `REQ-A-v1.0` was never applied to these answers.** [S-K: §VI.3] required that the request never ask a leading question. **The seven assertions were not produced by a neutral request, so they carry no neutrality guarantee.** They are recorded as the founder worded them and are not read as answers to questions nobody asked.
3. **20 of 36 sub-items are touched without a request having been issued.** The remaining 16 were never put to anybody. **A request is still required for those 16 — and `REQ-A-v1.0` remains `COMPOSED — NOT ISSUED`, now narrowed by §IV.3.**

# PART III — THE EVIDENCE CLASSIFICATION OF THE NEW INPUT

## III.0 Method

**[P]** Bound by **DA-1, DA-7, DA-9, DA-25, DA-26, DA-27, DA-28**.

**[P]** The mandate's central prohibition is adopted as the Part's structure: five conversions must not be performed silently. **Each is tested, and the test result is recorded so that the refusal is auditable.**

## III.1 The five prohibited conversions, each tested

| # | **Prohibited conversion** | **What the founder actually said** | **What the audit records** | **What the audit refuses to record** |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **1** | **"owns and controls" → legally incorporated ownership** | FA-6: *"Vasu Gupta owns and controls Krytos."* | **`FOUNDER-DECLARED OWNERSHIP AND CONTROL OF KRYPTOS (E1)`.** Two of the eight separated concepts are addressed, at the level of a personal assertion | **"Vasu Gupta is the legal owner of Krytos." "Vasu Gupta holds shares in Krytos." "Vasu Gupta is the shareholder/member/trustee/partner." No instrument, no register, no transfer, no share class, no beneficial-interest analysis exists** |
| **2** | **"India jurisdiction governs it" → completed Indian legal registration** | FA-3: *"India jurisdiction governs Krytos."* | **`JURISDICTION FOUNDER-ASSERTED: INDIA (E1); BASIS NOT STATED; REGISTRATION JURISDICTION NONE (derived from FA-4/FA-5)`** | **"Krytos is registered in India." "Krytos has a CIN, a PAN, a GSTIN or any identifier." "Indian company/trust/society/LLP law applies to Krytos." FA-3 states a governing jurisdiction; FA-4 and FA-5 state that no registration work exists. The two are recorded together and neither is stretched** |
| **3** | **"ecosystem head" → legally recognised parent entity** | FA-2: *"Krytos is also the ecosystem head for the things the founder is building."* | **`ARCHITECTURAL ROLE FOUNDER-DECLARED: ECOSYSTEM HEAD (E1). 0 corpus occurrences of the phrase. Not a legal term`** | **"Krytos is a parent company." "Krytos is a holding company." "Sewak Sathi is a subsidiary." **"Parent company" occurs 0 times in the corpus (H-PD-2) and the founder did not use it** |
| **4** | **"Virtual Brain" → legal person** | FA-1: *"Krytos is a Virtual Brain (VB)."* | **`CONCEPTUAL / OPERATIONAL IDENTITY FOUNDER-DECLARED: "VIRTUAL BRAIN (VB)" (E1). A new object-type outside the mandate's seven permitted legal classifications and outside the corpus's vocabulary`** | **"Krytos is a legal person of a kind called Virtual Brain." No such kind exists in the record, and DA-6 forbids supplying one from legal knowledge** |
| **5** | **"founder" → legally recognised shareholder / member / trustee / partner** | The mandate's framing: *"the founder has now explicitly supplied"*; **OC-07** previously established the founder role as a described role only | **`FOUNDER ROLE — DESCRIBED (E2, source-established) AND SELF-IDENTIFIED (E1). Surname now asserted. No office instrument exists`** | **Any of the five legal capacities. **[S-I: OC-07] recorded: *"A description and a name … No surname, no address, no office instrument."* The surname is now supplied; the office instrument still is not** |

**[P] Result: 0 of 5 conversions performed. All five refusals are recorded with the assertion they refuse to stretch.**

## III.2 What E1 permits and what it does not

**[P]** **[S-J: §III.6]** defines E1 as *"Personal assertion. A person states the fact"*, establishing *"Recording that the person said it"*, and not establishing the fact. **Rule J-6: it may open an acquisition item; it may not populate a constitutive field.** This audit applies that rule field by field, and records the one exception the corpus itself provides.

| Use | **Permitted at E1?** | **Authority** |
|---|---|---|
| Recording that the founder said it, in his words, with an evidence ID | **YES** | **DR-01 … DR-15; DA-4** |
| Opening, narrowing or closing an acquisition item | **YES** | **Rule J-6; §IV.3 closes A-03.2 and A-03.3** |
| Recording an **express negative** as `CONFIRMED ABSENT (E1)` | **YES — and this is the corpus's own provision, not a concession.** **[S-K: RS-3 `NONE`]: *"A deliberate negative, from a respondent"* establishes *"a deliberate negative"*, at *"E1 as to the statement; E2 if corroborated by an authoritative inventory"*. **A-03.3 was drafted precisely to receive it: *"including an express negative if no such document exists — `NONE` is a complete answer"*** | **[S-K: §VII.1 RS-3; §III.3 A-03.3]** |
| Answering a question of fact that requires no professional validation | **YES** | **[S-K: Rule K-59]: N-1 / KDP-01 / SA-01 — *"a question of fact, answerable by a person who knows"*** |
| Supplying the factual predicate for a professional question | **YES** | **Part XIX. This is what makes 19 of 52 PV questions `FORMULABLE`** |
| Populating a constitutive field — legal status, registration, ownership of an institution, governing authority | **NO** | **Rule J-6; K-4** |
| Establishing legal personality, jurisdiction as law, ownership as title, or the validity of any rule | **NO** | **K-3, K-4; DA-9** |
| Superseding any existing formulation or conflict | **NO** | **Rule K-53: `SUPERSEDED` requires a document at P3 and E4** |
| Moving any gate | **NO** | **DA-29. Part XVII: 0 of 10** |
| Authorising any activity | **NO** | **PP-01 … PP-09; Part XVIII list C** |

## III.3 The three separated authority fields — AS-1, AS-2, AS-3

**[P]** **[S-K: §X.1]** requires that three authority fields be kept separate and never merged. **K-PD-9 recorded that in all five cases examined the corpus had a fact source, no authority source and no validation source. That pattern is now broken at the first limb only.**

| Field | **Definition** | **Before this audit** | **Now** | **Movement** |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **AS-1 — the fact source** | Who knows the fact and can state it | **`NOT IDENTIFIED` for Krytos.** No holder named anywhere; **K-PD-1, K-PD-7, STOP-09** | **`IDENTIFIED AND HAS SPOKEN` — Vasu Gupta, `ASSERTED` standing, seven assertions received** | **`MOVED`** — the only field of the three that moves |
| **AS-2 — the authority source** | Who may decide, and on what instrument | **`AUTHORITY UNRESOLVED`** for DL-3, DL-4, DL-5, N-3, N-8, LDR-U01, A-N19, LDR-U19 **[S-F:159]** | **`AUTHORITY UNRESOLVED` — unchanged.** A disclosure is not a decision, and FA-6 does not constitute a deciding body. **[S-F: C-D-17]: *"the CEO cannot appoint the CEO, and nobody else is determined"*** | **`NO MOVEMENT`** |
| **AS-3 — the validation source** | Who may professionally verify, and whether commissioned | **`NOT COMMISSIONED`. 0 of 52 PV questions commissionable; 0 opinions held** | **`NOT COMMISSIONED` — but now `COMMISSIONABLE IN PRINCIPLE` for 19 of 52.** No professional has been instructed; no opinion exists; no fee is recorded | **`PARTIAL MOVEMENT`** — availability, not performance |

> **Rule DA-30 (No Authority By Disclosure).** **A person who supplies facts does not thereby acquire authority to decide what the facts mean.** The founder has answered AS-1. **AS-2 is not answered by the same act, and the audit does not treat it as answered.** Where the corpus recorded `AUTHORITY UNRESOLVED`, it still reads `AUTHORITY UNRESOLVED`.

# PART IV — KDP-01 … KDP-04, RE-RUN

## IV.0 Method

**[P]** Bound by **DA-7, DA-8, DA-9, DA-18, DA-26, DA-27, DA-28**.

**[P]** **[S-K: §XI.2]** supplies the update condition for each KDP verbatim, and Rule K-54 requires that updates be per-field. **Each KDP is therefore decomposed into its fields, each field is tested against its own update condition, and the KDP is not declared closed or open as a whole except where every field agrees.**

**[P] The permitted field values, declared before any field is tested:**

| Value | **Meaning** | **Level** |
|---|---|---|
| **`NOT ESTABLISHED`** | The record does not establish it, and makes no claim about the world | — |
| **`FOUNDER-DECLARED`** | The founder states it; nothing corroborates it | **E1** |
| **`CONFIRMED ABSENT (E1)`** | The only candidate holder expressly states that the thing does not exist | **E1** |
| **`SOURCE-ESTABLISHED`** | The corpus establishes it at a stated ID | **E2** |
| **`NOT STATED`** | The item was put and the response is silent on it — **never recorded as `NONE`** *(K-6)* | — |
| **`CONFLICTED`** | Two or more statements are incompatible and no authority has reconciled them | — |
| **`REQUIRES COUNSEL`** | A legal characterisation is needed and none exists | — |

## IV.1 KDP-01 — Krytos legal status

**[P]** Update condition, verbatim: *"Update only if evidence actually establishes legal status."* **[S-K: §XI.2]** applies the test: *"Has a document, at P3/E4, established a legal classification for a body identified as Krytos? An assertion does not establish it (K-4)."*

**[P] Test result: NO. No document exists. KDP-01 does not update as to legal status.** **But the mandate requires five distinct things to be tested separately, and four of the five now have values they did not have.**

| # | **The five things the mandate separates** | **Previous state ([S-I: Part III], [S-J: SA-01 … SA-05])** | **New evidence** | **Current state** | **Level** |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **1** | **Operational / conceptual identity** | **`NOT ESTABLISHED` as a definition.** H-Q01 `UNKNOWN`: *"five descriptions, none definitional"* — *"parent/company context and ownership ecosystem"* **[S: Ch.1]**, *"Owner / product ecosystem"* **[S: App. A §3.3]**, T2 PARENT, *"ownership ecosystem"*, *"product ecosystem"* | **FA-1 and FA-2** | **`FOUNDER-DECLARED` — Krytos is *"a Virtual Brain (VB)"* and *"the ecosystem head for the things the founder is building"*.** **A definition now exists where five descriptions existed.** Both terms occur **0 times** in the corpus (IN-U-9) and are recorded as new terms, not mapped onto existing ones (DA-28) | **E1** |
| **2** | **Claimed status** | **`NOT ESTABLISHED` — nothing was claimed.** 7 classifications tested, **0 selected**; the nearest approach was **[S-E: Rule E-58]**'s rationale calling Krytos *"a corporate owner"* — *"a rationale, not a decision"* **[S-H: C-2, NS-16]** | **FA-1, FA-4, FA-5** | **`FOUNDER-DECLARED: NO LEGAL STATUS IS CLAIMED.`** The founder claims a non-legal identity (Virtual Brain) and expressly disclaims any constitutive document or legal work. **Note the direction: the record previously could not say whether a legal status was claimed. It can now say that none is** | **E1** |
| **3** | **Legal status** | **`NOT ESTABLISHED`** — classification 7 returned | **FA-4, FA-5** | **`NOT ESTABLISHED` — UNCHANGED AS LAW.** The update condition fails: no document at P3/E4. **And now additionally: `NO LEGAL PERSONHOOD ASSERTED BY ANYBODY`.** [S-E: Rule E-58]'s *"corporate owner"* rationale is **not asserted by the founder** and stands as a rationale only. **`REQUIRES COUNSEL` — the legal effect of an unconstituted "Virtual Brain" in India is a legal question and is not answered here (DA-6)** | **E1 as to the negative; — as to law** |
| **4** | **Registration status** | **`NOT ESTABLISHED`.** `registered office` 0 · `CIN` 0 · `registry extract` 0 · `PAN` 0 · `GSTIN` 0 *(word-boundary, [S-I: §IV.1 row 3])* | **FA-4, FA-5 — by implication only** | **`NO IDENTIFIER ASSERTED; NO REGISTRATION WORK DECLARED (E1).`** The founder does not say "not registered"; he says no constitutive document exists and no legal structuring work has been completed. **A-01.4 is answered by implication and is flagged for direct one-word confirmation** — see §VI.2 item **U-CONF-1** | **E1, implied** |
| **5** | **Legal-person status** | **`NOT ESTABLISHED`** — the same as legal status, because no classification was selected | **FA-4, FA-5** | **`NOT CONSTITUTED — FOUNDER-DECLARED (E1).`** This is the field that moves furthest: the record can now state that **no constitutive act is declared to have occurred**, which is a stronger statement than *"the record does not know"* | **E1** |

**[P] The seven permitted classifications, re-tested (DA-6: no classification is chosen on plausibility):**

| # | **Classification** | **Previous result** | **Effect of FA-1 … FA-6** | **Current result** |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **1** | **LEGAL PERSON — TYPE ESTABLISHED** | `NOT ESTABLISHED` | **None.** FA-4 and FA-5 are express negatives | **`NOT ESTABLISHED`** |
| **2** | **LEGAL PERSON — TYPE UNKNOWN** | `NOT ESTABLISHED` | **Weakened, not established.** The founder claims no legal personality. **A claim is not evidence of the absence of a fact in the world, but it is evidence about the record, and the record now contains a denial** | **`NOT ESTABLISHED` — and no longer asserted by anybody** |
| **3** | **UNINCORPORATED ORGANISATIONAL PROJECT** | `NOT ESTABLISHED` for Krytos | **Not selected.** *"Virtual Brain"* is not *"unincorporated organisational project"*; the corpus uses *"unincorporated"* once, of a possible Sewak Sathi network **[S-C: HV-6]**. **DA-28 forbids the mapping** | **`NOT ESTABLISHED`** |
| **4** | **INFORMAL PROJECT / BRAND** | `NOT ESTABLISHED` — *"the corpus's descriptions are consistent with this reading"* | **Consistency increased, selection refused.** An unconstituted "Virtual Brain" that heads an ecosystem is *consistent with* an informal project or brand. **Consistency is not evidence (Rule I-16)** | **`NOT ESTABLISHED`** |
| **5** | **NATURAL PERSON'S BUSINESS / PROJECT** | `NOT ESTABLISHED` — *"the corpus contains statements pointing both ways"* | **Consistency increased, selection refused.** FA-6 places Krytos under a natural person's ownership and control. **That is a description of a relationship, not a classification of an object, and the mandate forbids choosing** | **`NOT ESTABLISHED`** |
| **6** | **OTHER** | `NOT ESTABLISHED` | **A candidate now exists and is recorded without being selected: *"Virtual Brain (VB)"* is, on its face, a status outside classifications 1–5.** Selecting it would require the founder or a professional to state it as a classification, and neither has | **`NOT ESTABLISHED` — CANDIDATE TERM RECORDED (E1)** |

>

>
> > ### *«Krytos's conceptual and operational identity is now established by founder evidence, while its legal status remains unestablished.»*
>
> **Four of KDP-01's five fields move. One — legal status as law — does not. No classification is selected. `REQUIRES COUNSEL` stands.**

**[P]** Update condition, verbatim: *"Update only if jurisdiction is actually established."* **[S-K]** applies the test: *"Has a source statement, corroborated, fixed a jurisdiction for Krytos itself — and distinguished it from any other person's?"* **Two limbs: fixed for Krytos itself — YES at E1. Corroborated — NO. The condition fails and the field records the assertion.**

| **2** | **State / province where relevant** | **`NOT ESTABLISHED`.** Indian states named only as Sewak Sathi operating environments — **[S-ST: §12.2]** Maharashtra; **[S-ST: §5 item 3]** *"state-specific trust/society regimes"*; **[S-E: LV-E-…]** | **None. FA-3 names a country, not a state** | **`NOT STATED`** — unchanged | **—** |
| **3** | **Registration jurisdiction** | **`NOT ESTABLISHED`.** `registered office` 0 · `CIN` 0 · `registry extract` 0 | **FA-4 and FA-5 — no constitutive document, no legal structuring work** | **`NONE — DERIVED FROM AN EXPRESS NEGATIVE (E1).`** There can be no registration jurisdiction where no registration work is declared to exist. **Recorded as derived, and flagged for direct confirmation (U-CONF-2)** | **E1, derived** |
| **4** | **Governing-law jurisdiction where established** | **`NOT ESTABLISHED`.** `governing law` and `governing-law`: **0 occurrences in the entire corpus** ([S-I: I-PD-3]) | **FA-3 asserts that India *"governs"* Krytos; FA-4 states no document exists** | **`FOUNDER-ASSERTED: INDIA (E1) — WITH NO DOCUMENT IN WHICH A GOVERNING LAW COULD BE RECORDED.`** The two assertions are compatible: a jurisdiction may govern a construct without any instrument saying so. **A-02.3 is answered `NONE` (no documented regime)** | **E1** |

**[P] A-02.2 — the basis for the jurisdiction — is `NOT STATED`, and its absence matters.** **[S-K: §III.2]** asked for *"place of formation, place of management, place of registration, or other"*. **Formation and registration are excluded by FA-4 and FA-5.** What remains is place of management, or the founder's own location, or *"other"* — **and none is stated.** [S-I: §IV.3] refused seven prohibited inferences that all converged on India; **FA-3 arrives at India by a route that is none of the seven, and that route is a person's statement.** The seven refusals stand and are not needed for this field.

|---|---|---|

| **Source-established jurisdiction** | **`NO`** | The corpus's 30 occurrences of `jurisdiction` remain 0 predicated of Krytos ([S-I: I-PD-3]); FA-3 is not in the corpus |
| **Professionally validated jurisdiction** | **`NO`** | 0 of 52 PV questions commissioned; no opinion exists |
| **Legally established jurisdiction** | **`NO`** | No constitutive document, no registration, no governing-law clause, no legal structuring work |

[S-A:2354]'s prohibition is unaffected and is reproduced: *"No legal conclusion is drawn from any of them in this document; each requires confirmation by a qualified professional for the actual entity, activity and jurisdiction."* **The entity is unconstituted, the activity is described, and the jurisdiction is now asserted. Two of the three are available; the first is a decision.** Part XIX.**

## VI.0 Method

**[P]** **[S-K]** built **PACK A (A-01 … A-05, 24 sub-items)** and **PACK B (B-01 … B-06, 12 sub-items)** as standalone sendable templates, and recorded **36 of 36 sub-items unanswered** at `RS-8 NO RESPONSE`. **Each is now re-tested against what actually arrived (DA-23: *"process only what actually arrived"*).**

**[P] Permitted per-sub-item results, declared before testing:**

| Result | **Meaning** |
|---|---|
| **`ANSWERED — E1`** | A positive statement was made and is recorded |
| **`ANSWERED — NONE (E1)`** | An express negative was made and is recorded |
| **`PARTIALLY ANSWERED — E1`** | Some of what the sub-item asks was stated; a named remainder was not |
| **`ANSWERED BY IMPLICATION — CONFIRMATION REQUIRED`** | The answer follows from another assertion but was not itself made |
| **`NOT STATED`** | Put, and the response is silent — **never recorded as `NONE`** *(K-6)* |
| **`NOT APPLICABLE`** | The sub-item's own terms no longer arise |
| **`NOT RECEIVED`** | The sub-item asks for an object that did not arrive |

## VI.4 The three confirmation items this audit creates

**[P]** Created because **DA-26** permits an express negative to close a request, and **DA-27** forbids extending a declaration to anything adjacent to it. **Each is one question, answerable in one word, requiring no document, no counsel and no decision.**

If none, please state NONE."* |

# PART VIII — R1 … R8 RECALCULATED

## VIII.0 Method

**[P]** Bound by **DA-4, DA-8, DA-11, DA-15, DA-17, DA-27, DA-28**.

**[P]** **[S-I: Rule I-27]** — *"Do not select any unless source evidence explicitly establishes it"* — and **Rule I-28** — *"`EXCLUDED` is returned only where a source instrument forecloses the model. The absence of support is not exclusion"* — are both carried forward and both bind here. **An E1 assertion is not a source instrument. No model is selected and no model is excluded.**

**[P] Permitted statuses, unchanged from [S-I: §X.0]:** **`EXCLUDED`** · **`STILL POSSIBLE`** · **`SUPPORTED BUT NOT SELECTED`** · **`SOURCE-SELECTED`**.

## VIII.1 The central question, answered first

> ### **Does *"Krytos is the ecosystem head to everything I am building"* select a relationship model?**
>
> ### **NO.**
>
> **Four reasons, each sufficient on its own:**
>
> 1. **It is not a legal statement.** *"Ecosystem head"* occurs **0 times** in the corpus (IN-U-9) and is not a term of any relationship model. R1 … R8 are defined in legal vocabulary — *"separate legal persons"*, *"owns and controls"*, *"subsidiary"*, *"holding"*, *"licensor"*, *"legally distinct entities"*. **A non-legal phrase cannot select a legal model without a conversion, and DA-28 forbids the conversion.**
> 2. **Its own speaker forecloses the legal reading.** FA-4 and FA-5, in the same breath, state that no constitutive document exists and no legal structuring work has been done. **R2, R3, R5, R6 and R7 each require at least one legal person called Krytos. On the founder's own declaration, none exists today.**
> 3. **It does not name Sewak Sathi.** FA-2 says *"the things the founder is building"*. **Sewak Sathi is not identified among them.** Rule I-28 and DA-27 forbid supplying the identification.
> 4. **Selection requires an authority, and the authority is unresolved.** H-Q28 — *"who may decide the Krytos relationship?"* — is `AUTHORITY UNRESOLVED`. **[S-F:1878] SR-01** assigns the entity-structure decision to Founder/CEO by a source-root assignment, which **[S-I: H-Q28]** recorded as *"a source-root assignment, not a legal authority"*. **A disclosure is not a decision (DA-30).**

Its **architectural** limb is contradicted at E1: FA-2 declares a relationship, and *"a historical reference with no present effect"* is not what the founder describes. **And its own premise — Sewak Sathi as a legal person — remains `UNRESOLVED` (`LDR-U08`)** | **`STILL POSSIBLE` — THE PRESENT LEGAL STATE BY ABSENCE; THE INTENDED ARCHITECTURAL STATE CONTRADICTED AT E1. | Status | **Previous ([S-I: §X.2])** | **Current** | **Movement** |

**[P] The mandate supplies candidate wording and permits its use only if the evidence supports it. It does.**

> ### **`ARCHITECTURAL RELATIONSHIP CLARIFIED (E1); LEGAL RELATIONSHIP NOT YET CONSTITUTED (E1).`**
>
> | Limb | **What is established, and by what** | **What is not established** |
> |---|---|---|
> | **Architectural** | **Krytos is the ecosystem-level conceptual and operational head of the founder's projects, and Sewak Sathi is described in the corpus as an operating organisation and product-ecosystem component within that context **[S: App. A §3.1]**. FA-1 and FA-2 supply the head's nature and role; the corpus supplies the component's description. Both are recorded at their own level: FA-1 and FA-2 at E1, the corpus description at E2** | That Sewak Sathi is among *"the things the founder is building"* — **not stated (DA-27)**. That the headship confers any right, power, entitlement or authority. That any of R1 … R8 obtains |
> | **Legal** | **That no legal relationship is constituted, on the founder's own express negative: no constitutive document (FA-4), no legal structuring work (FA-5)** | Everything. There is no legal person on the Krytos side; Sewak Sathi's legal structure is `LDR-U08 UNRESOLVED`; **0 of 8 models `SOURCE-SELECTED`**; **OS-1 … OS-7 unreconciled**; **`REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL STRUCTURE VALIDATION` applies to all eight models at HD-01, HD-03 and HD-27 — 24 cells ([S-H: §V.10])** |
>
> **This is a clarification, not a selection. It is recorded because the alternative — leaving the relationship wholly undefined — would misstate the record after a disclosure. It confers nothing.**

**[A] Finding U-PD-5 (new).** **The declaration moved two models and selected none, and the reason is structural: R1 … R7 are models of a relationship between legal persons, and the founder has declared that one side is not one.** **The eight-model frame built by v1.3-H assumes what FA-1 denies.** That is not a defect in the frame — the frame was built to enumerate the legal possibilities, and it still does. It is a finding about the present state: **the record's certified position, R8, is the only cell in the frame that can hold a non-legal head, and R8 was designed as the residual.** **Consequence, stated without softening: the architecture cannot select a relationship model until either (a) Krytos is constituted, or (b) a decision is recorded that the ecosystem head is and will remain a non-legal construct — in which case R1 … R7 all require re-derivation against a person, not a body. Both routes are decisions. Neither is an analysis. `REQUIRES FOUNDER DECISION` + `REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL STRUCTURE VALIDATION`.**

# PART X — GC-01, RE-RUN ON THE NINE-NODE TREE

## X.0 Method

**[P]** Bound by **DA-3, DA-6, DA-10, DA-12, DA-29**. **[S-I: Rule I-30]** — *"GC-01 must remain unresolved if its consequences cannot be professionally determined"* — binds. **[S-K: §XII.3]** supplies the impact vocabulary, which is used exactly:

| Level | **Definition ([S-K], verbatim)** | **What it permits** |
|---|---|---|
| **`GC-I-0` — `GC-01 IMPACT: NONE`** | The item bears on no node | Nothing |
| **`GC-I-1` — `PARTIAL`** | Supplies an input to one or more nodes without satisfying any closure condition | The node's *evidence available* field is updated; **the node does not close** |
| **`GC-I-2` — `MATERIAL`** | Satisfies a node's closure condition at the required level | **That node closes. The tree does not** |
| **`GC-I-3` — `POTENTIALLY CLOSING`** | Would close the tree, with validation and decision | **Nothing closes until validation and decision occur** |

**[P] The mandate's condition is adopted verbatim: *"Do NOT resolve GC-01 merely because the blueprint exists. The blueprint may establish the intended economic architecture without establishing its legal permissibility."* **The blueprint does not exist in the intake, so the condition is satisfied trivially — and the audit records that the condition would have bound even if the file had arrived.**

**[P] What the corpus preserves of the waterfall, quoted exactly, because GC-01 turns on it and because the mandate requires the source's own terminology (DA-4):**

ORGANISATION SERVICE FEE → 6. RESERVES / WAGE-PROTECTION BUFFER → 7. SOCIAL-IMPACT CONTRIBUTION WHEN CONDITIONS ARE MET → 8. RESIDUAL SURPLUS / FOUNDER UPSIDE** | **Diagram 14A, [appendix.txt:6301–6321]**; **[extracted_text.txt:3064–3088]** Ch.18; **[S-B:70]** FACT 4 |

**[A] Finding U-PD-8 (new).** **GC-01's root node closed on a negative, and a negative root is the one outcome the tree was not built to receive.** **[S-H: H-PD-14]** identified N-1 as *"the only node a human can close without a professional, a budget, a vote or an architectural change"*, and **[S-I: I-PD-18]** priced it: *"eight of the nine nodes are unreachable until N-1 is answered, and N-1 is the only node that costs nothing."* **The expectation encoded in the tree was that answering N-1 affirmatively would unlock the eight. Answering it negatively unlocks three (N-3, N-5, N-7) and forecloses five (N-2 becomes the sole root; N-4, N-6 and N-8 wait on it; N-9 is a self-assessment).** The consequence is recorded without softening: **the architecture's remaining foundational work is not disclosure. It is constitution — `SR-01`, a decision plus a counsel opinion — and every node except N-2 now waits on that one item.**

## XII.0 Method

**[P]** Bound by **DA-2, DA-10, DA-12, DA-14**. **[S-I: Rule I-33]** — *"Do not replace `UNRESOLVED` with 'two signatories'"* — and **[S-K: Rule K-56]** — *"Only a legitimate decision/validation process can change its status. Not a document. Not a repetition. Not a restatement. Not an assertion by the founder. Not a bank's general practice"* — **both bind, and Rule K-56 names this exact case.**

**[P] The five comparisons [S-K: §XII.2] requires before any status moves, applied:**

# PART XIII — I-PD-8 AND THE GOVERNANCE DEADLOCK, RE-RUN

## XIII.0 Method

**[P]** **[S-K: Rule K-57]** binds: *"The loop is recalculated, not assumed broken. Recalculation occurs only after evidence is accepted at ST-08 / FSM-09 — that is, provenance-checked, authority-checked, validated and reconciled. A supplied document does not trigger recalculation; an accepted one does."*

**[P] The seven assertions are not accepted evidence.** They are at P1 and E1; **FSM-09 is reached for 19 PV questions but no validation has occurred; FSM-10 is not reached; no decision is recorded.** **Recalculation is therefore not triggered, and this Part performs the recalculation as a test and records its result — which is that the loop does not break.**

**[A] Finding U-PD-10 (new).** **The deadlock is not an evidence deadlock, and the arrival of evidence proves it.** **[S-K]** classified three of the five nodes as *"not an evidence question"*, and this audit received evidence and re-tested all five: **DL-3, DL-4 and DL-5 did not move, because no statement by any person can supply a decider, a successor or a dispute mechanism to a body that does not exist.** The architecture has now spent six generations and one disclosure on a loop whose three hardest nodes were never waiting for disclosure. **What they wait for is constitution: a body, an instrument, and an authority that the instrument creates.** That is `SR-01`, and it is the same item that GC-01's remaining eight nodes wait on (U-PD-8), that all eleven pre-pilot validations wait on (J-PD-7), and that `A-N16`'s T-1 waits on.

# PART XIV — THE 240-CELL CONSEQUENCE MATRIX: DELTA ONLY

## XIV.0 Method

**[P]** Bound by **DA-3, DA-16, DA-27, DA-29**. **The mandate's instruction is adopted: *"Do not rewrite every cell unnecessarily."* This Part rewrites none of them.** It records a delta.

**[P]** **[S-H: Part V]** built a matrix of **8 models × 30 dimensions = 240 cells**, each carrying one of five mandated statuses, and counted it mechanically: **69 `SOURCE-SUPPORTED` · 35 `SOURCE-PROPOSED` · 57 `UNKNOWN` · 59 `REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL VALIDATION` · 20 `STRUCTURALLY DEPENDENT`**, with **83 mandatory-marker instances across 72 distinct cells**. **[S-H: §V.9]** also supplies the counting rule this Part depends on:

> *"Sub-markers — `REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL STRUCTURE VALIDATION`, `REQUIRES QUALIFIED TAX ADVICE`, `AUTHORITY UNRESOLVED`, `STRUCTURAL SEPARATION MAY BE REQUIRED — COUNSEL TO DETERMINE`, `UNKNOWN — REQUIRES GOVERNANCE COUNSEL`, `QUESTION FOR BROKER/COUNSEL` — are recorded **inside** a mandated status and are counted separately … **they do not change the 240-cell distribution above.**"*

**[P] Rule DA-32 (An E1 Assertion Is A Sub-Marker, Not A Status).** **A founder declaration is recorded inside a cell as the sub-marker `FOUNDER-DECLARED (E1)`, on the same footing as the six existing sub-markers. It does not change the cell's mandated status, because `SOURCE-SUPPORTED` requires *"the corpus contains an explicit statement supporting the cell's consequence, at a stated ID"* — and the seven assertions are not in the corpus.** This rule is what makes the delta countable and what prevents the matrix from being quietly rewritten by a disclosure.

**[P] The two-tier method, declared before any cell is examined:**

| Tier | **Cells** | **Treatment** | **Why** |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Tier 1** | **210 — R1 … R7** | **Premise-level annotation only. No cell re-run** | **All seven models' premises are founder-declaredly unsatisfied today (§VIII.3: 7 of 7). A hypothetical consequence of an unsatisfied premise does not change when the premise becomes *evidenced* as unsatisfied rather than merely unproven** |
| **Tier 2** | **30 — R8** | **Every cell re-run individually** | **R8 is *"the certified present state"* **[S-H: §V.8]**, and it is the only model whose premise the declaration satisfies (§VIII.2). Movement, if any, is here** |

| **2** | Tier 2 cells: **1 model × 30 = 30** |
| **3** | Tier 2 cells with an E1 sub-marker: **16** — the 12 partially resolved plus the 4 newly blocked |
| **4** | Tier 2 cells with no E1 input: **30 − 16 = 14** |
| **5** | Cells unchanged: **210 + 14 = 224**, plus **16 whose mandated status is unchanged but which carry a new sub-marker** = **240 cells with an unchanged mandated status** |
| **6** | Mandated distribution: **69 / 35 / 57 / 59 / 20 = 240 — UNCHANGED** |
| **7** | Cells moved: **0** |

**[P] Row 5 is where the two required categories overlap, and the overlap is declared rather than double-counted:** **240 cells are unchanged in mandated status; of those, 16 carry a new `FOUNDER-DECLARED (E1)` sub-marker, split 12 partially resolved and 4 newly blocked.** The mandate's five categories therefore read: **224 wholly unchanged · 12 partially resolved · 4 newly blocked · 0 moved · 59 requiring professional validation** — and **224 + 12 + 4 = 240 ✓**.

## XIV.4 What would change the labels

**[P]** Recorded because DA-32 is a rule about evidence level, not a permanent judgement, and because the corpus requires that the path out of a state be stated.

| If this arrived | **Then** | **Cells affected** |
|---|---|---|
| **The seven declarations embodied in an executed instrument, at P3 and E4** | The 16 sub-marked R8 cells would be re-tested for `SOURCE-SUPPORTED`, and OS-1 … OS-7 would become eligible for `SUPERSEDED` under Rule K-53 | **16 in R8; up to 210 in R1 … R7 via model selection** |
| **`SR-01` decided and a counsel opinion held — Sewak Sathi constituted** | **HD-01 and HD-03 would leave `UNKNOWN`/`REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL STRUCTURE VALIDATION`; HD-27 would become assessable; R1 … R7's premises would become evaluable rather than unsatisfied** | **24 across all models (HD-01, HD-03, HD-27 × 8) plus HD-27's dependants** |
| **One of R1 … R7 `SOURCE-SELECTED`** | **The other 210 cells would become counterfactual and the selected model's 30 cells would become the operative row** | **240 re-scoped to 30** |
| **The blueprint file received and reconciled at U17-R7** | **HD-15, HD-16 and HD-17 would acquire economic content; GC-01's N-4 and N-6 would become reachable** | **24 (HD-15, HD-16, HD-17 × 8)** |

**[A] Finding U-PD-11 (new).** **A disclosure that answers a question the architecture has asked six times can still move zero cells, and the reason is worth stating plainly: the matrix was built to record what the *corpus* supports, and a disclosure is not in the corpus.** This is not a defect and it is not a technicality. **If an E1 assertion could move a cell to `SOURCE-SUPPORTED`, then every ownership proposition in the corpus — which [S-J: §III.6] classifies as *"E1 assertions whose documents are at P0"* — would already have moved its cell, five generations ago.** DA-32 holds the line the corpus drew for itself. **The 16 sub-markers are the honest yield: real information, recorded at its real level, changing nothing it is not entitled to change.**

# PART XV — THE ECONOMIC ARCHITECTURE: A PRE-REGISTERED RECONCILIATION, NOT A PERFORMED ONE

## XV.0 Method, and why this Part is not the Part the mandate asked for

**[P]** Bound by **DA-5, DA-6, DA-10, DA-19, DA-23, DA-25**.

**[P] The mandate requires a direct source reconciliation of the supplied blueprint against twenty-six listed items, and requires that the actual supplied file be used as the authoritative source rather than remembered snippets. That instruction is correct and is not varied. It cannot be executed, because the file is not in the intake (§II.2).**

**[P] Three responses were available, and two are prohibited:**

| Response | **Permitted?** | **Why** |
|---|---|---|
| **Reconcile against remembered or reconstructed blueprint content** | **`PROHIBITED`** | The mandate forbids it expressly. **DA-5 and [S-I: Rule I-31]: *"Do not invent the Zero-Capital blueprint."* U17-R6 is `NOT MET — and prohibited`: the corpus's existing extractions at Ch.14 and Ch.21 are `[S]` as to *what v1.1 says*, never as to what the blueprint says (J-PD-2)** |
| **Return an empty Part** | **`PROHIBITED`** | **[S-I: Rule I-18]** — *The Extraction Is Performed On What Exists, And Marked As Not A Governing Document* — is the corpus's own alternative in operation: the twelve-point extraction was performed on what existed and every field was marked with what it rested on (§V.2), rather than left empty |
| **Pre-register the reconciliation** | **`PERMITTED, AND ADOPTED`** | Record, item by item: what the mandate asks; what the **corpus** preserves, at its own citation and level; which extraction category **EX-1 … EX-9** will be used; which corpus representation the blueprint will be checked against; and what a divergence would mean. **Nothing is reconciled. Nothing is extracted. No claim ID is issued** |

> ### **`RECONCILIATION STATUS: NOT PERFORMED — NOT PERFORMABLE. 26 OF 26 ITEMS RETURN `SOURCE NOT RECEIVED`.`**
>
> **Claims extracted: 0. Claim IDs issued: 0 — `CLM-01` remains unused. Economic conclusions drawn from the blueprint: 0. Numbers taken from the blueprint: 0.**

## XV.1 The seven comparison frames, defined now and populated on arrival

**[P]** The mandate specifies seven categories. **Each is defined with a decision rule, so that no category is applied by judgement when the file arrives.**

| Frame | **Definition** | **Decision rule** | **What it may never be used for** |
|---|---|---|---|
| **A — EXACT MATCHES** | The blueprint states what the corpus preserves, in terms that do not differ materially | **Both texts quoted side by side. A match requires the same rule, the same direction and the same qualifier. A match on the rule with a different qualifier is frame E, not frame A** | Adopting a figure. A match at E3 does not make a figure final |
| **B — MISSING ELEMENTS PREVIOUSLY ASSUMED FROM SUMMARIES** | Something the corpus's instruments treated as present in the blueprint, which the blueprint does not contain | **Every instance must name the instrument and the sentence that assumed it. [S-B:40] is the first candidate: *"preserves the blueprint principles as they appear inside the attached PDF and the founder's present instructions"*** | Treating an absence as a prohibition. **Silence is not permission (Rule I-19), and it is equally not a prohibition: `NOT ESTABLISHED` *"records the state of the evidence and makes no claim about the state of the world"* (Rule I-4)** |
| **C — NEW ELEMENTS** | Something in the blueprint that no corpus instrument records | **Recorded at EX-1 … EX-9 with its own qualifier. **A new element is not a new rule: [S-F: Rule F-9] — a proposal that has not been approved has no operative force*** | Immediate adoption. **[appendix.txt:684–685]**: *"If a later full blueprint contains additional controls, it should be incorporated by controlled amendment rather than silent replacement"*** |
| **D — CONTRADICTIONS** | The blueprint states the opposite of, or is incompatible with, a corpus statement | **Issued as a new `CF-` row. **Not resolved inside this instrument ([S-K: §X.2]). Both texts preserved with both citations (DA-15, DA-16)*** | Preferring the blueprint because it is newer (Rule I-23) or preferring the corpus because it is `APPROVED` |
| **E — EXPLICITLY ILLUSTRATIVE RATHER THAN FINAL** | The blueprint attaches a qualifier to its own content | **The qualifier is quoted verbatim and travels with the figure permanently (Rule K-49). **The corpus's own model instance is [appendix.txt:6364–6369]*** | Stripping the qualifier. **A figure quoted without its qualifier is a fabricated figure** |
| **F — REQUIRING PROFESSIONAL VALIDATION** | The item cannot be deployed without counsel, a CA, a labour professional, a broker or a data-protection professional | **Assigned to a `PV-` question, existing or new. **[S-A:2354]**: every external source *"requires confirmation by a qualified professional for the actual entity, activity and jurisdiction"*** | Treating a `[V]` marker as satisfied by the blueprint's own existence |
| **G — REQUIRING FOUNDER APPROVAL** | The item the corpus reserves to the founder | **[appendix.txt:6367–6369]**: *"Final percentages, caps, multipliers and calculations require founder approval and professional validation."* **[S: Ch.19 step 8]**: *"FINAL MULTIPLIER REQUIRES APPROVAL."* **Both limbs are required; neither alone suffices** | Treating a founder statement as the approval. **A disclosure is not a decision (DA-30)** |

**[A] Finding U-PD-12 (new).** **The reconciliation is fully specified on one side and entirely empty on the other, and that asymmetry is the finding.** For all twenty-six items the corpus target exists, is cited and is quotable — the eight priorities, the definitions, the control table, the illustrative figures, the professional categories, the gate conditions. **What is missing is not knowledge of what to compare; it is the thing to compare it with.** When the file arrives, this Part executes without further design: **EC-01 … EC-26 provide the item list, EX-1 … EX-9 provide the extraction discipline, frames A … G provide the classification, `CLM-nn` provides the claim register, and Rule K-49 provides the qualifier discipline.** **No step will need to be improvised, and improvisation is where an economic reconciliation would otherwise go wrong.**

**[P]** Bound by **DA-10**. **The mandate's classification vocabulary is used exactly, and is mandatory for every important numerical item:** **`SOURCE BLUEPRINT FIGURE`** · **`ILLUSTRATIVE`** · **`PILOT TARGET`** · **`ARCHITECTURAL CONTROL`** · **`PROPOSED`** · **`FINAL/APPROVED`** · **`REQUIRES VALIDATION`**. **An item may carry more than one classification; where it does, all are recorded and the primary is named.**

**[A] Finding U-PD-13 (new).** **The corpus's own treatment of its three most quotable numbers is the discipline this Part is built to preserve, and it is unusually explicit.** **[appendix.txt:6364]** is a section heading whose entire content is a refusal: *"14.4 Illustrative percentages are not final policy"*, followed by *"This specification does not adopt those as final Sewak Sathi Sanstha percentages. Final percentages, caps, multipliers and calculations require founder approval and professional validation."* **The source anticipated the exact failure mode that receiving the blueprint would create — a reader promoting 37.5 / 17 / 12 into policy because a document finally arrived.** The protection survives the arrival of the document, and it survives twice over here, because the document did not arrive.

# PART XVII — PILOT GATE RE-EVALUATION

**[P]** Bound by **DA-11, DA-13, DA-20, DA-29**. **The mandate's prohibition is adopted verbatim: *"Do NOT assume that receiving the blueprint authorises the pilot."* **Nothing was received, and had it been, the prohibition would have bound.**

**[P]** **[S-F: Rule F-14]** supplies the gate's own arithmetic: *"All ten sub-gates must PASS. A PASS requires evidence, not assertion."* **The evidence required for each sub-gate is named in the source and is reproduced below. An E1 assertion is not the evidence any of the ten names.**

> ### **`THE PILOT IS NOT AUTHORISED. NOTHING HAS MOVED FROM BLOCKED TO READY.`**
>
> **What has moved is not a gate. Four things moved, and each is recorded at its own level:**
>
> 1. **Two gates now fail on evidence rather than on silence** — P0-A and P0-C. **A gate that fails on an express negative is closer to being actionable than one that fails on an absence, because the action is identified: constitute the entity.**
> 2. **One node of GC-01 closed** — N-1, negatively.
> 3. **Nineteen of fifty-two professional questions became formulable** — the first time Rule K-58's condition ④ has passed.
> 4. **`STOP-09` cleared** — a person with standing exists and has spoken.
>
> **None of the four is a PASS condition for any of the ten sub-gates, and DA-29 forbids recording any of them as one.**

# PART XVIII — WHAT WE ARE ALLOWED TO BUILD NOW

## XVIII.0 Method

**[P]** Bound by **DA-11, DA-20, DA-29**. **The mandate's test for list A is adopted verbatim: *"Only items whose prerequisites are genuinely satisfied."* **An item enters list A only where this audit can name the prerequisite, name the evidence that satisfies it, and name the gate that does not apply. **Anything else is list B or list C.**

**[A] Finding U-PD-14 (new).** **List A has six items and list C has thirty-four, and the ratio is the finding.** Five generations produced lists in which list A was empty, because nothing had arrived and no person was identified. **List A is non-empty now, and every one of its six items is an act of records, of transfer, of speech or of instruction — not one is an act of the organisation.** That is not a limitation of this audit. **It is the accurate shape of an institution that has no legal person, no account, no officer, no constituted body and no pilot: the only acts available to it are acts by the person who declares he owns it.** Whether that person may act *for* the organisation, rather than *as himself*, is `SR-01`, and it is `REQUIRES COUNSEL`.

# PART XIX — THE PROFESSIONAL VALIDATION HANDOFF, RE-RUN

## XIX.0 Method

**[P]** Bound by **DA-6, DA-11, DA-12, DA-21, DA-30**. **[S-K: Rule K-58]** is applied verbatim:

> *"A question becomes commissionable only when **all four** hold: **① the necessary evidence exists · ② the relevant professional scope is known · ③ the question is sufficiently specified · ④ no missing foundational source prevents meaningful review.**"*

**[P] A new distinction is required, because the corpus's single word *"commissionable"* conflates two things that this audit separates:**

| State | **Definition** | **Test** |
|---|---|---|
| **`FORMULABLE`** | The question can now be written down with real facts, a named jurisdiction and a specified professional scope | **Rule K-58's ①, ②, ③ and ④ all pass, at the level the question requires** |
| **`COMMISSIONABLE`** | The question can be put to a professional and an answer obtained | **`FORMULABLE`, plus an instructing party, plus a fee, plus — where the corpus requires it — a document** |
| **`COMMISSIONED`** | A professional has been instructed | An instruction exists and is recorded |
| **`ANSWERED`** | A written opinion is held as governance evidence | **[S-F:1975] SR-98**: *"Written opinions held as governance evidence"* |

|---|---|---|---|

For questions whose necessary evidence is a *fact about Krytos*, the fact now exists at E1. **For questions whose necessary evidence is a *document*, it still fails — and FA-4 establishes that no such document exists to fail with*** | **`PASSES AT E1 FOR 19 OF 52`** · **`FAILS FOR 33 OF 52`** · **`FAILS AS TO EVERY DOCUMENT-DEPENDENT QUESTION`** |

[S-A:2354]: every external source *'requires confirmation by a qualified professional **for the actual entity, activity and jurisdiction**'* — and none of the three is known"** | **All three are now known, at E1.** **The actual entity: none is constituted — FA-4, FA-5, and `LDR-U08 UNRESOLVED` for Sewak Sathi. ## XIX.4 The instructing party

**[P]** **[S-K]** recorded that commissioning *"still requires an instructing party, and none is constituted (J-PD-17)"*. **That finding is preserved, and it is now qualified by a fact this audit is required to state because it is the difference between a blocked path and an available one.**

| Question | **Answer** | **Basis** |
|---|---|---|
| **Is a constituted instructing party available?** | **NO.** There is no entity, no board, no council, no appointed CEO, no finance function. **[S-F: C-D-17]**: *"the CEO cannot appoint the CEO, and nobody else is determined"* | Unchanged from **[S-K]** |
| **Is a natural person available who can instruct a professional?** | **YES — at E1.** FA-6 and the mandate's framing identify **Vasu Gupta**. **A natural person does not require an entity, a bank account, a constituted body, a budget approval or a vote in order to obtain legal advice about forming one** | **U-PD-9** |
| **Does the corpus contemplate this?** | **YES.** **[S-F:175]** proposes *"an ENTITY-STRUCTURE DECISION PAPER, **for the founder/CEO**, putting the options to qualified Indian corporate and tax counsel"*. **[S-F:1878] SR-01** names the owner as **Founder/CEO** and the evidence required as *"a written decision **plus counsel opinion**"* — the opinion precedes the decision. **[S-F:1891] SR-14**: *"Search now — cheap, and irreversible if lost"* | **[S-F]** |
| **May the organisation pay for it?** | **`NO — A-N16 REMAINS BLOCKED.`** No account exists; no mandate exists; **PP-04**: *"A payment made informally is not a mandate; it is an unauthorised payment"* | Part XII |
| **May the founder pay personally?** | **`REQUIRES FOUNDER DECISION` + `REQUIRES QUALIFIED TAX ADVICE.`** A personal payment for organisational advice has related-party, contribution and deductibility implications that this audit does not assess (DA-6). **No fee, budget or funding source is recorded anywhere in the corpus, and none is invented (DA-10)** | **N-7, CF-16** |
| **Is instruction therefore authorised?** | **`AUTHORISED AS AN ACT OF A PERSON, NOT AS AN ACT OF THE ORGANISATION.`** It is list A item **A-4**, and the qualification is part of the authorisation | §XVIII.1 |

| **J-PD-7** | *All eleven pre-pilot validations across eight professional categories depend on SR-01, whose next action has been performed four times* | **PRESERVED, AND THE COUNT IS NOW SIX.** v1.3-G, v1.3-H, v1.3-I, v1.3-J, v1.3-K and this audit have each produced the entity-structure material for counsel. **SR-01's next action has been performed six times and SR-01 has never been advanced, because producing a paper is not instructing a professional** |

**[A] Finding U-PD-15 (new).** **Annexure C acquired a jurisdiction and did not acquire an entity, and [S-A:2354] requires all three.** The corpus's own rule is unusually precise: an external source *"requires confirmation by a qualified professional **for the actual entity, activity and jurisdiction**"*. **FA-3 supplies one of the three. The activity was always supplied. The entity is the one that is missing, and it is missing in a new way: not because nobody knows what it is, but because the founder has said that nothing has been constituted.** That converts the professional question from *"which of these ten options fits?"* to *"nothing exists; here is what we intend; what should be created?"* — **which is the question SR-01 has asked since v1.3-F, and which is now answerable.**

# PART XXI — NEW FINDINGS: U-PD-1 … U-PD-17

## XXI.0 How these are numbered, and what they are not

**[P]** Bound by **DA-15, DA-16, DA-25**. **Finding IDs are assigned in discovery order across the audit and are never re-used, never renumbered and never transferred between Parts** — the failure mode that produced the I-PD-11 … I-PD-14 collision in v1.3-I. **Two IDs were reserved during drafting — `U-PD-3` and `U-PD-9` — and are stated in full below, in their reserved positions. A seventeenth finding, `U-PD-17`, was discovered while writing the verdict and is stated at §XXIV.4; it is included in this Part's totals.**

> ### **A FINDING IS NOT A RESOLUTION.**
>
> **None of the seventeen findings recorded in this Part closes a KDP, an open question, a conflict, a gap, a decision node, a matrix cell, a gate, a stop condition or a register row. Each records something the audit discovered that the corpus did not contain, and each is classified at its own evidence level. Where a finding looks like progress, the level says otherwise: 15 of the 16 rest on E1 assertions or on the audit's own analysis, and none reaches E3.**

## XXI.1 The register

| ID | **Finding** | **Stated at** | **Level** | **What it does not do** |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **U-PD-1** | **The blueprint exists in three distinguishable states at once and the corpus has no vocabulary for the third.** State 1: `SOURCE-REFERENCED — NOT SUPPLIED` (**[S-B:40]**, **DOC-13**). State 2: `ASSERTED SUPPLIED — NOT PRESENT IN INTAKE` (**FA-7**). State 3 would be `RECEIVED`. **States 1 and 2 are not the same, and the corpus's RS-1 … RS-8 could not express state 2 — so `RS-9` was minted** | §II.2, §III.1 | **E1 for the assertion; E0 for the content** | **Does not recover INPUT-05. Does not establish that the file exists. Does not permit extraction** |
| **U-PD-2** | **A declaration of absence converts an acquisition problem into a constitution problem.** For five generations the missing Krytos documents were framed as documents to be located, obtained and hashed (**DOC-13, DOC-16 … DOC-19, SR-14, `LDR-U17`, the whole of Part II of v1.3-J**). **FA-4 removes that framing for one category: there is nothing to acquire, because nothing was created.** The work is not retrieval; it is creation | §II.5 | **E1** | **Does not close SA-03 or SA-04 as to Sewak Sathi, whose status is `UNRESOLVED` and not `CONFIRMED ABSENT`. Does not make N-2 a decision. Does not authorise any entity form** |
| **U-PD-3** | **The architecture has acquired an object type it has no register for.** Four searches returned **0 corpus occurrences** of *"Virtual Brain"*, *"VB"*, *"ecosystem head"* and *"Gupta"*. **The corpus registers entities, assets, roles, artefacts, documents, measurements, decisions and obligations — and has no register for a declared non-legal coordinating construct that is asserted to head an ecosystem.** The nearest existing objects are the National Council (**`PROPOSED`, unconstituted**), the apex body (**`AUTHORITY UNRESOLVED`**) and a brand (**`A-N12 UNRESOLVED`**), and none of them is this. **The consequence is structural, not terminological: `R1 … R8` are eight models of legal relationship, and a non-legal head is not a value of any of them — which is why R8 is the only row in Part VIII whose cells can carry the assertion at all (§XIV.2)** | §II.1, §VIII, §XIV.2 | **E1** | **Does not create the register. Does not classify Krytos as an entity, a trust, a brand, a project, a holding construct or a person. Does not decide whether such a head is permissible — `REQUIRES PROFESSIONAL STRUCTURE VALIDATION`** |
| **U-PD-4** | **Four of the five foundational inputs moved and none completed, and 0 of 5 reach E3.** INPUT-01 and INPUT-02 `PARTIALLY RECOVERED`; INPUT-03 `EXPRESS NEGATIVE`; INPUT-04 `NOT RECOVERED`; INPUT-05 `NOT RECOVERED`. **21 of 31 SA rows touched; 0 KDPs closed; 10 fields moved.** The pattern is consistent: **assertions answer the *who* and the *where*, and never the *what instrument*. Every field that a document would settle remains `NOT ESTABLISHED`** | §V | **E1** | **Does not close KDP-01 … KDP-04. Does not reach E2 for any input, because an internal record of a founder's assertion is still an assertion** |
| **U-PD-5** | **`R1 … R7` all presuppose a legal person; only R8's frame can hold a declared non-legal head.** The seven relationship models are models of legal relationship — subsidiary, division, brand, project, affiliate, associate, separate entity. **FA-2's *"ecosystem head"* is asserted to be none of these, and is asserted not to be a legal person at all.** The result is that the corpus's central structural question has acquired a candidate answer that its own option set cannot express: **0 models excluded, 3 still possible, 5 supported but not selected, 0 source-selected** | §VIII | **E1** | **Does not select a model. Does not add a ninth model. Does not decide that a non-legal head is workable — that is `SR-01`'s question for counsel** |
| **U-PD-6** | **The evidence machine was entered from outside.** FSM-01 … FSM-11 describes `REQUEST → RECEIVE → VERIFY → VALIDATE → DECIDE → UPDATE`. **No request was issued, so FSM-03 was bypassed and the machine was entered at FSM-04. AUD-03 could not record what happened, because it records issuances — so `AUD-11 UNSOLICITED DISCLOSURE RECEIVED` was created.** This is a real case the protocol did not contemplate, and the corpus's own rule anticipated it in the negative: **Rule K-1, `REQUEST ≠ EVIDENCE`, protects against treating a request as evidence; it says nothing about evidence arriving without a request** | §XX.3, §XX.4 | **The audit's own analysis, E2** | **Does not retrospectively issue a request. Movement: **1 MOVED, 6 PARTIALLY MOVED, 1 TRANSFORMED, 1 EXTENDED, 19 NO MOVEMENT.** The dependency re-computation gives **9 + 0 + 8 + 6 + 5 = 28** | §IX | **E1** | **Does not close any question. # PART XXIII — THE NO-FALSE-CLOSURE AUDIT

## XXIII.0 Method

**[P]** Bound by **DA-14, DA-17, DA-24, DA-25**. **The mandate's requirement is mechanical: *"no silent closure, no invented resolution"*.** This Part tests every class of object the corpus can close, states how many were closed, and — for each closure that did occur — names the authority that closed it and the level at which it closed.

> ### **THE TEST APPLIED TO EVERY ROW**
>
> **A closure is legitimate only if three things can be named: ① the object closed · ② the evidence or authority that closed it · ③ the level at which it closed. Where any of the three cannot be named, the object is not closed and the row says so.**
